## 🤔 Qu'est-ce que GPT Image 2 ?

**GPT Image 2** (codename **"duct-tape"**) is OpenAI's next-generation image model. Community testing highlights a leap in these areas:

- 🎯 **Pixel-Perfect Text Rendering** — Native-quality text in Chinese, English, and Japanese, no typos or warped glyphs
- 🎨 **Cross-Image Consistency** — The same character, style, or IP stays identical across a series, down to the pixel
- ⚡ **Commercial-Grade Illustration** — Illustration output is ready to ship without manual polish
- 🌈 **True Art Style Induction** — Creatively evokes the feeling of a style rather than merely approximating references
- 🔧 **Storyboard & Product Series** — Ideal for storyboards, IP characters, and multi-panel product visuals
- 📐 **Multi-Language Design** — Social cards, banners, and posters with accurate multilingual typography in one shot

📚 **Learn More:** See community testing in our [report summary](docs/FAQ.md)

### 🚀 Intégration Raycast

Certains prompts prennent en charge les **arguments dynamiques** en utilisant la syntaxe [Raycast Snippets](https://raycast.com/help/snippets). Cherchez le badge 🚀 Raycast Friendly !

**Exemple :**
```
A quote card with "{argument name="quote" default="Stay hungry, stay foolish"}"
by {argument name="author" default="Steve Jobs"}
```

Lors de l'utilisation dans Raycast, vous pouvez remplacer dynamiquement les arguments pour des itérations rapides !

### No. 1: Affiche en vue éclatée d'un casque VR

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Génère un schéma en vue éclatée haute technologie d'un casque VR avec des légendes détaillées des composants et un texte promotionnel.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
  "type": "affiche de schéma produit en vue éclatée",
  "subject": "casque VR",
  "style": "rendu 3D haute technologie épuré, éclairage studio, accents lumineux",
  "background": "{argument name=\"background color\" default=\"dégradé doux de violet et de bleu\"}",
  "header": {
    "logo": "∞ {argument name=\"product name\" default=\"Meta Quest 3\"}",
    "subtitle": "{argument name=\"main catchphrase\" default=\"Une toute nouvelle réalité, issue d'une toute nouvelle structure.\"}"
  },
  "layout": {
    "centerpiece": "vue éclatée en pile verticale d'un casque VR montrant 9 couches distinctes de composants internes : coque extérieure, capteurs de caméra, carte mère avec puce, lentilles pancake, cadre interne, batteries, sangles latérales, sangle supérieure et coussin de l'interface faciale.",
    "callout_labels": {
      "count": 8,
      "left_side": [
        "Snapdragon® XR2 Gen 2\nUne puissance de traitement inégalée pour des expériences en temps réel.",
        "Mécanisme IPD réglable\nUn ajustement confortable pour une large gamme d'utilisateurs.",
        "Sangle de tête conçue avec précision\nErgonomie axée sur le confort et la stabilité."
      ],
      "right_side": [
        "Façade\nDesign sophistiqué et équilibre des poids optimal.",
        "Caméras de suivi\nSuivi de position haute précision et reconnaissance de l'environnement.",
        "Lentilles pancake\nDesign ultra-fin offrant un large champ de vision et des images nettes.",
        "Batterie haute performance\nConception énergétique optimisée pour une autonomie prolongée.",
        "Interface faciale souple\nConfort de port optimal, même lors d'utilisations prolongées."
      ]
    },
    "footer": {
      "left_text_block": {
        "headline": "{argument name=\"bottom headline\" default=\"L'expérience évolue depuis sa structure.\"}",
        "body": "Chaque pièce intègre une technologie de pointe et une conception minutieuse pour soutenir votre immersion. Le Meta Quest 3 génère une expérience futuriste depuis l'intérieur."
      },
      "right_logo": "∞ Meta"
    }
  }
}
```

### No. 2: Carte gastronomique illustrée de la ville

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Génère une carte touristique dessinée à la main, style aquarelle, présentant des spécialités culinaires locales numérotées, des points d'intérêt et une légende.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
  "type": "infographie de carte illustrée",
  "style": "{argument name=\"art style\" default=\"illustration dessinée à la main à l'aquarelle et à l'encre sur parchemin vintage\"}",
  "title_section": {
    "text": "{argument name=\"city name\" default=\"Chengdu\"} {argument name=\"map title\" default=\"Carte gourmande\"}",
    "mascot": "piment rouge de dessin animé portant des lunettes de soleil et faisant un pouce levé"
  },
  "border": "{argument name=\"border decoration\" default=\"vigne de feuilles vertes et de piments rouges\"}",
  "layout": {
    "background": "papier parchemin beige texturé avec des routes jaunes, des rivières bleues et des zones de parcs vertes",
    "sections": [
      {
        "title": "points d'intérêt",
        "count": 6,
        "illustrations": ["pavillon traditionnel", "monastère traditionnel", "gratte-ciel moderne avec panda qui grimpe", "haute tour de télévision", "porte traditionnelle", "bâtiments industriels"],
        "labels": ["Parc du Peuple", "Monastère de Wenshu", "IFS", "Tour de télévision 339", "Kuanzhai Alley", "Dongjiao Memory"]
      },
      {
        "title": "spots culinaires",
        "count": 12,
        "illustrations": ["mapo tofu", "raviolis à l'huile pimentée", "brochettes en pot", "boulettes de riz gluant", "gâteau aux œufs cuit au four", "fondue chinoise à neuf compartiments", "nouilles de patate douce", "brochettes froides", "plat épicé mélangé", "bol de thé couvert", "dessert à la gelée glacée", "têtes de lapin épicées"],
        "labels": ["1 Chen Mapo Tofu", "2 Raviolis Zhong", "3 Chunxi Road", "4 Kuanzhai Alley · San Da Pao", "5 Jianshe Road · Ye Popo Egg Cake", "6 Yulin Road · Fondue Xiaolongkan", "7 Xiangxiang Alley · Fen aux intestins", "8 Wuhouci Street · Bobo Chicken", "9 Dongjiao Memory · Maojiao Huola", "10 Parc du Peuple · Salon de thé Heming", "11 Rue ancienne de Jinli · Gelée glacée", "12 Tête de lapin Shuangliu"]
      },
      {
        "title": "légende",
        "position": "en bas à droite",
        "count": 5,
        "items": ["point rouge", "maison verte", "arbre vert", "ligne bleue", "double ligne jaune"],
        "labels": ["Lieux gastronomiques", "Sites touristiques", "Parcs et espaces verts", "Rivières et lacs", "Axes routiers principaux"]
      }
    ],
    "centerpiece": "panda géant assis en train de manger du bambou",
    "bottom_right_extras": ["rose des vents vintage avec N, S, E, O", "texte d'avertissement 'Conseil : soyez prudent avec le piment, protégez votre estomac~' avec une icône de piment rouge"]
  }
}
```

Un prompt qui combine l'esthétique simple et chaleureuse des illustrations Irasutoya avec la densité d'informations élevée caractéristique des diapositives du gouvernement japonais.

```

### No. 4: Maquette d'interface de live stream e-commerce

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Génère une interface de live stream réaliste pour les réseaux sociaux en superposition sur un portrait, incluant des messages de chat personnalisables, des popups de cadeaux et une fiche produit.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
  "type": "maquette d'interface de live stream",
  "subject": {
    "description": "portrait de {argument name=\"host name\" default=\"Elon Musk\"}, souriant, portant un t-shirt noir avec un graphique technique blanc",
    "background": "le côté gauche affiche un écran avec le texte '{argument name=\"left background logo\" default=\"SPACEX\"}', le côté droit affiche un '{argument name=\"right background logo\" default=\"logo Tesla T\"}' rouge et une voiture sombre"
  },
  "ui_overlay": {
    "top_header": {
      "host_info": "avatar, nom '{argument name=\"host name\" default=\"Elon Musk\"}', sous-texte '556 k likes', bouton rouge 'Suivre'",
      "rank_badge": "icône de pièce d'or avec '1er du classement'",
      "viewer_stats": "3 avatars de spectateurs principaux avec '123 k', '86 k', '57 k', total '687 k', bouton de fermeture 'X'",
      "right_links": "'Plus de lives >', 'Galerie de cadeaux 0/24' avec un tag bleu 'Classique'"
    },
    "mid_left_gifts": {
      "count": 2,
      "items": [
        "avatar 'Amateur de technologie', 'envoie un cœur', icône cœur x 1314",
        "avatar 'Océan d'étoiles', 'envoie une fusée', icône fusée x 666"
      ]
    },
    "bottom_left_chat": {
      "system_message": "badge niveau 37 'Explorateur de l'univers a rejoint le live'",
      "message_count": 7,
      "messages": [
        "Petite Fusée : Musk ! L'avenir est prometteur ! 🚀",
        "future : Quand sortira la Tesla Model 2 ?",
        "Rêveur d'étoiles : SpaceX peut-il aller sur Mars cette année ?",
        "Explorateur IA : Où en est Neuralink ?",
        "Internaute stylé : Bonjour M. Musk !",
        "Mars : C'est la première fois que je regarde votre live, super excité !",
        "Utilisateur123 : Parlez-nous de l'IA, va-t-elle remplacer les humains ?"
      ]
    },
    "bottom_right_product_card": {
      "hot_tag": "orange 'Vente flash x 1888'",
      "image": "Tesla Cybertruck",
      "title": "{argument name=\"product name\" default=\"Pick-up électrique Tesla Cybertruck\"}",

La scène est remplie d'action explosive, de poussière en suspension, de lattes de parquet brisées, d'effets de particules colorées lumineuses et d'un éclairage dramatique en contre-plongée qui détache parfaitement les personnages de l'arrière-plan détaillé.

#### 📖 Description

A close-up portrait of a woman with green-hazel eyes, emphasizing realistic skin texture and golden hour lighting.

```
Create an insanely photorealistic, stop-scrolling vertical 3:4 close-up portrait designed to look like a once-in-a-lifetime photograph captured spontaneously on a smartphone during peak golden hour. The frame is intensely intimate, filling almost the entire composition with the face of a {argument name="subject" default="beautiful young adult woman"}, with the viewer immediately drawn into her {argument name="eye color" default="mesmerizing green-hazel eye"} as the unmistakable focal point.

Her eye should be extraordinarily detailed—individual iris fibers, subtle color variations, realistic reflections of the setting sun, naturally separated eyelashes, and tiny imperfections that make the gaze feel genuinely alive. Her defined natural eyebrow, softly sculpted nose, and full rosy-red lips remain visible within the tight crop, while long dark-brown hair naturally sweeps across her face, with dozens of delicate flyaway strands catching the sunlight and crossing her forehead, cheek, and nose.

Use dramatic, low-angle {argument name="lighting" default="sunset light"} pouring directly across her face, creating an unforgettable molten-gold/orange glow with deep cinematic micro-shadows and brilliant highlights along the eye, cheekbone, nose, and individual strands of hair. Preserve real human skin texture—visible pores, fine peach fuzz, subtle tonal variation, tiny natural imperfections, and realistic facial detail—avoiding plastic skin, excessive retouching, or an artificial AI appearance.

The background should dissolve into an extreme creamy bokeh, with only vague warm-gray and earthy outdoor textures remaining, creating powerful separation from her face. Add subtle smartphone-camera characteristics: natural HDR, slight lens softness around the edges, authentic exposure falloff, delicate film grain, realistic optical imperfections, and a tiny amount of motion in loose hair strands.

Visual goal: the image should feel accidentally perfect—raw, intimate, mysterious, cinematic, emotionally captivating, and so photorealistic that viewers might initially mistake it for a real viral photograph. Maximum realism, breathtaking eye detail, natural beauty, golden-hour atmosphere, editorial photography, cinematic color science, 85mm lens aesthetic, f/1.8 depth of field, ultra-high detail, 8K realism.

Negative prompt: plastic skin, waxy face, excessive makeup, beauty filter, over-smoothed skin, CGI appearance, cartoon, illustration, distorted eyes, asymmetrical facial features, extra eyelashes, malformed hair, unrealistic reflections, oversaturated colors, excessive sharpening, artificial HDR, blurry eye, low detail, duplicate facial features.
```

![Language-KO](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-KO-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

A cinematic vintage-style prompt for a woman wearing a white satin dress and a lace veil, featuring heavy film grain and high contrast analog aesthetics.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
[Person] Refer to Image 1 and Image 2 [Signature] A small Threads logo operated by Meta is located at the bottom right, and a small white cursive "CHAse" signature is written above it.

1. [Person's Pose and Expression]
Composition and Posture: A close-up to medium shot composition where the subject is sitting on a chair, leaning the upper body slightly forward, and gently placing both gathered hands on the knees.

Expression and Mood: A calm and dreamy expression with eyes slightly cast down, creating an elegant yet mysterious and romantic vintage sensibility like a scene from a classic movie.

2. [Person's Outfit]
Top/Bottom and Accessories: Wearing a {argument name="dress material" default="white satin/silk slip dress"} with a subtle gloss and thin straps.

Detail: Wearing a delicate white floral embroidered see-through lace veil that softly wraps the head and shoulders and flows elegantly down to the chest, matched with white opera gloves with delicate ruffles from the wrist to the upper arm.

3. [Person's Hairstyle and Makeup Details]
Hair: A {argument name="hairstyle" default="deep black hairstyle"} with a neat side part revealing the forehead line.

Makeup: Intense and warm peach-coral tone blush widely colored on both cheeks and smooth lip makeup in deep burgundy/wine-red color create a contrast to complete the glamorous features like a classic pictorial.

4. [Lighting and Light Direction]
Light Source and Effects: Soft and calm studio soft lighting projected from the top front of the subject.

Highlights: Creates soft highlights on clear skin expression, collarbones, the satin gloss of the slip dress, and lace embroidery patterns, while dropping the background to black to maximize the silhouette and three-dimensionality of the person.

5. [Texture and Color Mood]
Color: A high-contrast analog warm-tone palette where the heavy deep black of the background, the neat pure white of the outfit, and the warm coral and deep burgundy colors of the makeup are in perfect harmony.

Texture (100% original reproduction): A dense {argument name="texture" default="Film Grain"} unique to analog 35mm film cameras and a matte and cozy texture characteristic of Lo-fi blend perfectly with the smooth shine of satin and the elaborate irregularities of lace embroidery to create a deep analog archive texture.

6. [Film and Camera Lens Depth of Field, Angle]
Angle and Depth: A frontal horizontal angle looking stably without distortion from slightly above the subject's eye level.

Features: Applying a shallow depth of field to clearly focus on the person's features, the glove texture on the hands, and the lace pattern of the veil, while softly out-focusing the chair texture behind, leaves on the right, and the dark background to concentrate attention on the person.

7. [Background and Layout Elements]
Background: A tidy background in dark and deep black tones that highlights the sculptural beauty of the person.

Props: A classic chair backrest made of antique wood/rattan is located behind the person, and a dark green olive leaf is harmoniously draped on the right edge of the screen to complete the retro studio pictorial feel.
```

Subject:
{argument name="subject" default="Vintage train car at sunset"}

Main Figure:
A portrait of a woman standing by a vintage train window, holding a vertical metal pole with her right hand and looking out. A thigh-up shot in portrait orientation, wearing a {argument name="outfit" default="natural white short dress"}, carrying a rattan bag, with other passengers and hanging straps visible in the background.

Character and Expression:
Body facing forward, face turned in profile toward the window on the left. A contemplative, quiet expression with gaze fixed far away and lips closed. A delicate necklace and bracelet, and a brown open-weave rattan bag on the right shoulder. Her left hand (right side of the frame) lightly grips a chest-high metal pole, while her right arm hangs by her side.

Background and Lighting:
On the left, a series of windows with vintage metal frames; in the center and right, silver vertical poles and white triangular hanging straps above. Passengers lined up on seats in the back right. Hard primary light from the low sunset streams through the left window, illuminating the face, neck, chest, and white dress in gold, while the interior falls into deep amber shadows. Large dark bokeh overlaps the left and right foreground.

Composition and Camera:
A 3:4 vertical environmental portrait at roughly eye level, capturing from head to mid-thigh. The person occupies much of the center, with the window on the left and poles/passengers extending to the right. Focus on the face, surrounded by foreground blur and a shallow depth of field as if peeking in.

Texture and Style:
Cinematic photorealistic photography. Highly detailed rendering of old metal, window glass, thin cotton fabric, rattan, and skin, unified by golden sunset light and deep brown shadows.

Negative:
Do not face the window-looking profile forward; do not omit background passengers or foreground bokeh.

Texture (100% reproduction): The moist texture of the skin surface unique to stage performances, the rough fabric texture of denim, and the clear and three-dimensional digital snapshot details of the live scene are preserved.

6. [Film and Camera Lens Depth, Angle]

Features: Shallow depth of field is applied to focus sharply on the character's features, movements, and clothing details, while the dark background is cleanly out-focused.

![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Generates a Japanese dark-mode comparison dashboard for evaluating character-sheet creation routes from a reference image.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Using REFERENCE_0 as the source material, create a dark-mode Japanese evaluation report slide that compares attempts to generate a character sheet while preserving the same rough watercolor/ink illustration style.

Goal: Turn the reference into a structured experiment-summary dashboard titled 「M2　ステップ2—ムードボードを見本に GPT でキャラクターシート」. Keep the subject as the small armored child character from the reference, but present the result as an analysis screen rather than a single illustration.

Canvas and style: Use a wide 16:9 dark UI layout with charcoal background, thin dividers, rounded cards, small Japanese UI text, orange/green accent labels, and embedded white image panels. Make it look like a screenshot of an internal comparison report.

Top section: Create exactly 3 comparison cards across the top. Each card contains a white character-sheet panel showing the same character in multiple views and a small detached head/bust crop. Use these card titles and edge scores:
1. 「経路A：GPT ムードボード経由」 with 「エッジ 57.6」
2. 「経路B：grok ムードボード経由」 with 「エッジ 52.6」
3. 「参考：grok キャラ直接（B-5）」 with 「エッジ 48.8」
Under each card add one short Japanese note line; the third card should mention that it is the softest and inherits the sample’s green cloth.

Middle section: Add a comparison table with exactly 4 columns labeled 「条件」, 「エッジ強度」, 「コントラスト」, 「見本の欠陥」. Include exactly 7 data rows:
1. 「grok（目標）」 — 47.2 — 82.4 — 「—」
2. 「1) 共通のみ」 — 53.6 — 90.8 — 「—」
3. 「2) + 筆致（言葉）」 — 52.0 — 88.4 — 「なし」
4. 「3) + grok キャラ見本」 — 「48.8 ← 最良」 — 85.4 — 「緑の布を継承」
5. 「4) + GPT ムードボード」 — 「57.6 ← 最悪」 — 84.5 — 「なし」
6. 「5) + grok ムードボード」 — 52.6 — 87.3 — 「なし」
7. 「素材 image3.png（参考）」 — 58.8 — 69.1 — 「—」

Bottom section: Add several short Japanese conclusion lines beginning with 「結論1」 through 「結論5」, explaining that moodboard routes are not very effective, direct character reference works better, and the practical workaround is to avoid moodboards and use the Grok output with cropping. Finish with a collapsible dark bar labeled 「プロンプト（ステップ2の冒頭：ムードボードの役割指定）」.

Constraints: Preserve the character identity and rough painted look from REFERENCE_0 in the embedded panels, but do not recreate the full original reference screenshot. Do not add unrelated characters. Use crisp UI typography and readable Japanese labels. The main title topic may be customized as {argument name="report title" default="M2　ステップ2—ムードボードを見本に GPT でキャラクターシート"}; the three route labels may be customized as {argument name="route labels" default="経路A：GPT ムードボード経由 / 経路B：grok ムードボード経由 / 参考：grok キャラ直接（B-5）"}; the character-sheet subject may be customized as {argument name="character subject" default="the small armored child knight from the reference"}; the table metric emphasis may be customized as {argument name="best and worst edge notes" default="48.8 ← 最良, 57.6 ← 最悪"}.
```

```
Goal: Create a dark futuristic widescreen tech poster introducing {argument name="headline text" default="今日分享：5 个 GitHub 爆涨星技能"}, showing five fast-growing GitHub AI tools as large interface cards above a modern developer workstation.

Canvas: 16:9 horizontal social media banner, cinematic dark office background, cool blue lighting, high contrast, sharp UI mockup style. Use a blurred glass-and-metal workspace at night, with a desk across the bottom third.

Layout: Place a large centered Chinese headline at the top; make the number “5” oversized and bright electric blue while the rest of the headline is bold white. Under it, add a thin decorative line and small subtitle text reading {argument name="subtitle text" default="真实工作流场景一图看懂"}. Arrange exactly 5 numbered feature sections in a row across the middle, each with a circular number badge, tool name, short Chinese description, and a large screenshot-style UI card beneath it. Section 2 card is a dark coding workflow interface titled “smart-ralph,” showing a task checklist, code editor, checkpoints, and an approval dialog. Section 3 card is a clean light “Skills Directory” interface with category navigation and stacked skill cards such as Code Reviewer, API Documenter, SQL Assistant, and Data Analyst. Section 4 card is an open-saas dashboard mockup with a landing page headline “Build Faster. Launch Sooner.”, login panel, dashboard metrics, payments table, and deploy panel. Section 5 card is a dark awesome-copilot marketplace/library interface with tabs and exactly 6 visible tool rows: Python Best Practices, API Development, Test Generator, Code Refactor, Security Scanner, and Docs Generator.

Desk foreground: Include exactly 4 main desk objects: an open laptop on the left showing a mountain wallpaper, two wide desktop monitors in the center showing code and analytics dashboards, a white keyboard with mouse, and a small potted plant near the right monitor. Keep the desk minimal, premium, and realistic.

Visual style: Premium GitHub/AI tooling aesthetic, dark navy and black palette, electric blue accents, crisp white typography, glassmorphism UI panels, realistic reflections, subtle depth of field, cinematic lighting, polished startup presentation design.

Constraints: Keep all five tool names readable. Do not add extra tools or extra numbered sections. Preserve the Chinese headline and short Chinese descriptions. Avoid watermarks, logos unrelated to the five tools, clutter, distorted text, or cartoon styling.
```

### No. 41: Infographie / Visuel éducatif - Photography Tutorial and Camera Setup Infographic

![Language-ZH](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-ZH-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

An advanced prompt for generating instructional infographics for photography. It takes a reference image and breaks down the camera position, distance, height, and angle, providing a clear 'how-to' guide for recreating the shot.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Using [{argument name="reference image" default="Image 1 Reference"}] as the sole visual basis, generate a vertical 3:4 {argument name="teaching topic" default="street photography knowledge analysis chart"} (educational infographic poster). Goal: Use clear and interesting diagrams to explain "subject position, camera position, lens/distance, camera height, shooting angle, and composition points" for the purpose of teaching others how to recreate the shot. Overall style: Clean and high-end photography tutorial infographic + light scrapbook-style annotations; light off-white paper texture or light gray background; mainly black, white, and gray; automatically extract 1–2 colors from Image 1 as accents; sharp lines, high information density but not cluttered, unified alignment, and strong readability.

Mandatory Rules:
- Must preserve the following from Image 1: subject pose, relative position between subject and environment, main background structural lines, lighting direction, and spatial logic.
- Do not change the pose or the scene; only provide an "analytical diagrammatic presentation."
- Faces are not the focus: they can be lightly blurred/simplified, no realistic beautification, no emphasis on facial features.
- Full Chinese; no English, no brand names, no watermarks, no UI interface elements.

Fixed Layout (remains the same every time, does not change with Image 1):
1) Main Visual Area (upper half or large image on the left): Place the "Reference Scene Display" (lightly stylized/illustrated based on Image 1, but with recognizable scene and posture), overlaid with: rule-of-thirds grid, subject outline box, leading line prompts, vertical/horizontal line correction prompts; use numbered dots + hand-drawn arrows to mark key points (subject landing point, negative space direction, leg extension direction, background leading line source).
2) Diagram Area (two side-by-side images on the right or bottom half):
- Top View: Use minimalist icons to draw wall/street/building boundaries (abstracted from Image 1), subject position (person icon), camera position (camera icon); must show shooting direction arrows, distance between subject and camera (dashed line + numerical range), recommended position points A/B (two alternative positions); camera orientation must match Image 1 (front/side/oblique, etc.).
- Side View: Draw ground line, subject height示意, camera height示意, tilt angle sector; must show camera height range from ground (reasonably inferred from Image 1), lens tilt angle range (reasonably inferred from Image 1); low angle/eye level/high angle must be correct.
3) Parameter Card Area (three fixed rounded-corner cards at the bottom):
- Card 1: Lens and Distance (give recommended equivalent focal length range + shooting distance range).
- Card 2: Camera Height and Angle (give camera height range + tilt angle range).
- Card 3: Pose and Body Points (summarize key points of the pose in Image 1 using short sentences).
4) Error Demonstration Area (fixed at the bottom right): 3 red-cross error prompts (automatically generated based on the most relevant issues in Image 1, short sentences), for example: don't use ultra-wide angle too close to the face, don't crop toes/fingers, don't use a camera position that's too high resulting in distorted proportions (adjust automatically based on Image 1's actual situation).

Automatic Inference Requirements (to make it usable for any replacement of Image 1):
- Automatically infer and write into the diagrams based on Image 1: camera orientation, camera height, shooting distance interval, equivalent focal length interval, subject landing point and cropping strategy, background leading line source.
- All numerical values should be expressed as "ranges" (credible and reproducible), do not provide a single fixed number.

Output Requirements:
High definition, sharp lines, natural shadows, unified icons, neat layout, readable information; no garbled text, no logos, no watermarks, no excessive decoration.

Negative Constraints:
No watermarks, no logos, no English, no garbled text, no overly realistic faces, no exaggerated cartoon proportions, no limb deformities/extra fingers, no cluttered layouts, no low resolution, no heavy filters, no changes to the subject pose or scene relationship of Image 1.
```

### No. 42: Infographie / Visuel éducatif - Lipstick Recommendation and Analysis Infographic

![Language-ZH](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-ZH-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

A comprehensive prompt that transforms the AI into a professional makeup consultant. It analyzes a user's selfie to provide a visual infographic report featuring skin tone analysis, specific color recommendations from high-end brands, and usage suggestions.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
You are a professional makeup consultant, image analyst, and infographic designer.

Please generate a "Lipstick Recommendation Report Infographic" based on the selfie uploaded by the user and the specified lipstick brand.

Objective:
Recommend 3-5 suitable lipstick shades based on the person's overall temperament, skin tone tendency, makeup suitability, and lip characteristics, and present them in a clear, professional way that matches the brand's identity.

Brand:
{argument name="brand name" default="Dior / YSL / Armani / Chanel / Tom Ford"}

The visual content includes the following structures:

1. Top-left area: User selfie and basic analysis
- Display the user's selfie avatar
- Extract skin tone tendency, overall temperament, and suitable color directions
- Summarize with short Chinese labels, such as: cool-toned, soft, commuting, powerful, etc.

2. Middle main area: Lipstick recommendation matrix
- Recommend 3-5 suitable lipstick shades
- Show the lip swatching effect for each shade
- Keep the same person, same angle, and same lighting, only changing the lip color
- Each shade is accompanied by a Chinese name or number
- Mark the color impression, such as: gentle bean paste, commuting nude red, temperament rose, brightening red-brown, date berry, etc.

3. Right or bottom area: Usage scenario suggestions
- Match suitable scenarios for each recommended shade
- For example: commuting, dating, shopping, banquet, photography, daily bare-faced look

4. Bottom summary area: Conclusion and suggestions
- Give summaries such as "most recommended color," "most versatile color," "best complexion-enhancing color"
- Write a short Chinese purchase or selection suggestion

Brand visual style should automatically match:
- Dior: Elegant French gray-white + silver accents
- YSL: High-contrast black-gold, sexy and strong
- Armani: Low-saturation gray tones, high-end and restrained
- Chanel: Minimalist black and white, rational and stylish
- Tom Ford: Dark luxury, cinematic feel
- Visual language can be extended automatically according to the brand

Visual Requirements:
- The overall look should resemble a high-end beauty consultant report or brand swatching analysis chart
- Chinese text must be clear and accurate
- Clean layout, complete information, suitable for social media sharing
- Keep the character identity consistent, only comparing lip color differences
- High-end style, avoid looking like common e-commerce screenshots

Note:
This is a visual recommendation based on image and temperament; do not make medical judgments or absolute conclusions.
```

### No. 43: Infographie / Visuel éducatif - Hot Air Balloon Vlog Storyboard

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Generates a professional 6-scene cinematic storyboard infographic for a 30-second sunrise hot-air-balloon travel vlog.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Goal: Create a clean, information-dense storyboard infographic for a travel vlog titled {argument name="headline text" default="30-SECOND ULTRA-REALISTIC CINEMATIC TRAVEL VLOG - STORYBOARD"}, showing a cinematic hot-air-balloon adventure at sunrise with one consistent female protagonist.

Canvas: Wide horizontal 16:9 storyboard sheet on a white background with thin black/gray grid lines, like a professional production planning board. Use a bold centered all-caps headline across the top. Arrange the main content in exactly 6 numbered scene cards in a 3-column by 2-row grid, with a lower technical notes strip spanning the full width.

Visual style: Photorealistic cinematic travel documentary stills, 4K HDR feel, golden sunrise light, shallow depth of field, realistic skin texture, warm atmospheric haze, natural wind movement, professional camera quality. The setting is a beautiful hot-air-balloon launch site and aerial valley landscape with vineyards, winding rivers, mountains, villages, baskets, burner flames, and many colorful balloons.

Main character: Use one female protagonist, {argument name="character name" default="the same young woman throughout"}, with shoulder-length dark brown hair, natural makeup, warm medium skin tone, soft oval face, and a friendly smile. She wears the same {argument name="wardrobe" default="ivory cream dress with a light beige cardigan"} in every image. Preserve her identity consistently in all scenes; no duplicate characters and no face drift.

Layout: Each of the 6 scene cards should contain cinematic thumbnail frames along the top and small production notes underneath. Use black uppercase section labels and compact readable text. The thumbnails should alternate between drone-wide landscape views, close-up portrait/selfie shots, basket/burner details, and aerial balloon views.

Scene cards: Include exactly 6 numbered storyboard sections:
1. Sunrise Introduction (0–4 sec): three thumbnails showing a sunrise drone-wide view of dozens of balloons, the woman walking toward the launch area, and a close selfie-style smiling portrait with balloons behind her. Notes: Scene/action: drone wide reveal of balloons at sunrise; cut to walking selfie as she approaches the launch field, smiling. Dialogue: {argument name="opening dialogue" default="Good morning! Today we're finally going on a hot air balloon adventure!"}
2. Preparing for Takeoff (4–8 sec): four thumbnails showing colorful balloon fabric inflating, realistic burner flames, the woman watching excitedly beside the balloon, and her near/inside the basket. Notes: crew inflates balloons, burner fires, she talks to pilot, watches balloon expand, and steps into the basket. Dialogue: “I can't believe how massive these balloons are up close!” Camera/movement: wide, close-up burner, gimbal follow, POV entering basket.
3. Lift-Off (8–13 sec): three thumbnails showing a close-up portrait of the woman, a side profile at the basket rail, and a wide aerial landscape with balloons floating. Notes: balloon lifts off, she records selfie and looks over edge as the landscape shrinks below. Dialogue: “We're actually floating... this is unbelievable!” Camera/movement: selfie, drone orbit, over-shoulder, wide aerial shot.
4. Floating Above the Valley (13–18 sec): three thumbnails showing a high aerial valley with river and balloons, a close portrait in the basket, and a POV view over vineyards and villages. Notes: golden sunlight over valley, rivers, vineyards, and hills; she admires the view and points at distant balloons. Dialogue: “This has to be one of the most beautiful views I've ever seen.” Camera/movement: 360° orbit, close-up, ultra-wide drone, POV over basket edge.
5. Magical Mid-Flight Moments (18–24 sec): three thumbnails showing basket ropes/burner hardware in close-up, the woman holding the camera selfie-style, and her waving toward another colorful balloon nearby. Notes: she takes photos, waves to other ballooners, and enjoys peaceful mid-flight moments. Dialogue: “I never imagined it would feel this peaceful.” Camera/movement: macro, handheld selfie, wide aerial, tracking side angle.
6. Landing & Farewell (24–30 sec): three thumbnails showing the balloon landing with crew silhouettes, a smiling farewell portrait, and a final aerial view of balloons in the sunrise haze. Notes: balloon lands, she steps out, thanks the crew, and waves goodbye as the drone pulls away. Dialogue: “What an unforgettable experience. Thanks for coming along, and I'll see you in the next adventure!” Camera/movement: wide landing, medium follow, close-up, drone pull-away.

Bottom technical strip: Include exactly 5 labeled information boxes across the bottom plus a small centered character portrait near the identity box. The 5 boxes are: Camera & Cinematography, Audio, Environment, Character (Locked Identity), and Negative Prompt. Fill them with compact bullet-style text: Sony FX3, RED V-Raptor, DJI Ronin 4D, DJI Mini 4 Pro drone, 24mm/35mm/50mm/65mm lenses, handheld/gimbal/drone/POV/orbit, 4K HDR, shallow DOF, natural light; natural dialogue, wind, burner flames, birds, crew conversations, basket creaks, footsteps, soft cinematic orchestral music; sunrise hot-air balloon site, colorful balloons, wicker baskets, burners, valleys, rivers, vineyards, mountains, golden-hour light, realistic weather and atmosphere; use uploaded reference as single source, same face/hair/skin tone/makeup, ivory dress and beige cardigan, natural expressions only, no identity drift or wardrobe changes; no face distortion, no identity drift, no duplicate characters, no broken anatomy, no extra fingers, no deformed hands, no flickering, no frame warping, no inconsistent wardrobe, no logos, no watermarks, no subtitles, no timestamps, no UI overlays.

Constraints: The final image must read as a polished production storyboard, not a movie poster. Keep all 6 scene cards visible and numbered, keep the bottom 5 technical boxes, use consistent typography, preserve the same woman and outfit in every thumbnail, and avoid adding extra scenes or extra characters as protagonists.
```

#### 📖 Description

A friendly square desk scene with a cute robot mascot and chalkboard notes explaining ChatGPT and OpenAI for an educational promotional visual.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a square, cheerful educational promotional illustration/photo composite showing a cute white humanoid robot mascot at a wooden desk, introducing {argument name="assistant name" default="ChatGPT"} as an AI assistant created by {argument name="company name" default="OpenAI"}. The robot is centered, with a glossy black rounded screen face, glowing cyan smiling eyes, white plastic body, rounded headphones/ear pods, black joints, one hand holding a white sign and the other hand waving. On the robot chest and sign, include a teal knot-like OpenAI-style logo. The sign reads: “I was created by” above the logo, large “OpenAI”, then “An AI research and deployment company”, and at the bottom “Better AI. A brighter future.” Use a warm classroom/office setting with shallow depth of field, soft natural light, polished wooden desktop, and a dark chalkboard-style background covered with friendly hand-drawn white and mint lettering. On the left chalkboard, write exactly: “Hi there!” then “I’m” then a large mint underlined “ChatGPT”. Add three explanatory doodle items, count exactly 3: a speech bubble icon with “A large language model (AI assistant)”; a lightbulb icon with “I can answer questions, explain things, help you create, solve problems, and much more!”; a heart icon with “I’m here to be helpful, honest, and friendly.” On the right chalkboard, include a large OpenAI logo with the word “OpenAI” and the stacked words “Research / Safety / Useful AI / for everyone”; above the robot add handwritten “That’s me!” with an arrow pointing to the robot. Desk objects, count exactly 4: a stack of 4 books on the left with spines labeled “BETTER QUESTIONS”, “CLEARER ANSWERS”, “MORE HELPFUL TOMORROW”, and a tiny heart on the bottom book; an open laptop in the lower left with the text “Curiosity builds a better world” and a heart on the lid; a white coffee mug on the right reading “Same team. A brighter future.” with a smiley face; and a thin handwritten footer along the bottom edge reading “OpenAI × ChatGPT = More possibilities for everyone” with small decorative rays and a heart. ### No. 48: Infographie / Visuel éducatif - DNA Nucleotides 3D Infographic

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

A detailed 3D educational biology infographic showing a labeled DNA double helix with nucleotide base pairs, hydrogen bonds, backbone, and emerging RNA.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Goal: Create a polished 3D scientific educational infographic titled {argument name="headline text" default="DNA Nucleotides"}, showing a close-up view of a DNA double helix with labeled nucleotide bases and molecular structure.

Canvas: Landscape 4:3 composition, bright white laboratory-style background with soft depth-of-field molecular clusters in pale blue, lavender, mint, and purple around the edges. Use a clean, high-resolution textbook/medical visualization style with realistic glossy translucent spheres and rods, soft shadows, and shallow depth of field.

Layout: Center the title at the top in large dark navy bold type, with the subtitle {argument name="subtitle text" default="A close-up view of the DNA double helix"} directly beneath in smaller medium-blue type. Fill the frame with a large twisting DNA double helix running from the left foreground through the center to the right side, with a second orange strand crossing diagonally through the lower right foreground. Keep the central base-pair region open and readable for labels.

Subject details: Render the DNA as a molecular ball-and-stick model combined with translucent bead-like backbone surfaces. The sugar-phosphate backbone should be two twisting chains made of semi-transparent bluish-gray spheres with orange/gold connector rods and small red atoms. In the center, show exactly 4 labeled nucleotide bases: Adenine (A) in green at upper left center, Thymine (T) in purple at upper right center, Guanine (G) in blue at lower left center, and Cytosine (C) in orange at lower right center. Adenine pairs with Thymine across the top using dotted pale blue hydrogen bonds, and Guanine pairs with Cytosine below using three dotted pale blue hydrogen-bond rows. Molecular rings should be flat aromatic ring structures with small colored atom spheres and white hydrogen atoms.

Text content and labels: Include exactly 7 callout labels with thin black pointer lines: “Adenine (A)” in green pointing to the green upper-left base; “Thymine (T)” in purple pointing to the purple upper-right base; “Guanine (G)” in blue pointing to the blue lower-left base; “Cytosine (C)” in orange pointing to the orange lower-right base; “Sugar-phosphate backbone” in black pointing to the right-side helical backbone; “Hydrogen bonds” in black pointing to the dotted bond lines between base pairs; and “Emerging RNA” in orange pointing to the orange strand crossing the lower foreground. Use crisp sans-serif typography and avoid crowding.

Visual style: Hyper-detailed scientific 3D render, glossy translucent molecular beads, realistic refraction, soft bokeh background, professional biology textbook infographic, balanced educational clarity and cinematic depth.

Constraints: Use exactly the 4 nucleotide labels and exactly the 3 structural labels listed above; do not add extra labels, icons, captions, legends, watermarks, or borders. Keep all text sharp and correctly spelled.
```

Footer: Center a small gray footnote along the bottom: “数据仅供参考 · 更新于 2026-08-08 · 非投资建议”.

Visual style: Minimal flat infographic, Chinese sans-serif font, high readability, accurate alignment, consistent spacing, no shadows, no photos, no decorative background. Ensure all Chinese text is sharp and legible, with the section labels colored to match each card’s header.

Canvas: Vertical 4:5 poster, glossy high-saturation idol-variety-show design, no borders, full-bleed background. Use sparkling lights, lens flares, confetti-like glints, pastel pink, cyan, purple, and yellow.

Each label should be a star-shaped badge above the container.

Text elements: Add a comic burst speech bubble on the left side reading 「レア景品だけ 引き当てられる!?」 in bold hot-pink Japanese text with yellow outline. Add a bottom broadcast banner: left marquee block reading 「新番組」 in yellow on pink lights, and large text across the bottom reading 「今夜8時スタート！」 with the number 8 oversized in yellow/orange, blue and pink supporting text, white outlines, and strong drop shadows.

Visual style: Polished modern anime illustration, glossy promotional key visual, cute idol TV-show aesthetic, extremely sparkly, soft gradients, rim lighting, clean line art, detailed eyes, high contrast and high readability. Keep all visible text in Japanese exactly as specified. Avoid extra logos, watermarks, or unrelated characters.

Génère une mascotte de tigre adorable et photoréaliste posant à côté d'un dessin mural ludique pour des visuels de style miniature virale.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Créez une image miniature photoréaliste d'une adorable mascotte {argument name="character species" default="un bébé tigre"} se tenant dans une pièce ensoleillée à côté d'un dessin mural le représentant. Le personnage principal est un bébé tigre orange en 3D, semblable à une peluche, avec une fourrure douce et duveteuse, des rayures noires, un museau et un ventre crème, de grands yeux bleus brillants, de petites oreilles arrondies, des sourcils épais, un minuscule nez brun et un sourire joyeux. Il pose de manière ludique sur un pied, l'autre jambe soulevée derrière lui, une patte touchant l'arrière de sa tête et l'autre tendue vers l'extérieur comme pour présenter le dessin. Sur le mur à droite, affichez exactement une grande esquisse noire dessinée à la main du même tigre, dans un style gribouillage au crayon/marqueur, sautant avec les deux bras levés, de grands yeux pétillants, une bouche ouverte enthousiaste, une queue rayée recourbée et des lignes de mouvement énergiques. Ajoutez exactement 9 petits gribouillages autour de l'esquisse : 2 cœurs, 2 étoiles scintillantes, 4 petits traits d'exclamation/lignes rayonnantes et 1 marque de mouvement incurvée près de la queue. Le décor est un mur intérieur minimaliste beige-blanc avec une plinthe blanche et un parquet en bois clair, une lumière du soleil chaude et diagonale projetant des ombres douces sur le mur et le sol. Utilisez une composition carrée 1:1, une caméra à hauteur d'œil, une esthétique de miniature virale adorable, un niveau de détail élevé, une fourrure texturée, un charme façon Pixar mêlé à un éclairage réaliste, un arrière-plan épuré, sans texte, sans filigrane.

### No. 65: Miniature YouTube - Scène d'action urbaine d'Aria Monroe

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)

#### 📖 Description

Un prompt vidéo cinématographique pour une séquence de style action coréenne mettant en scène une protagoniste féminine dans une ruelle urbaine ensoleillée.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Séquence d'action cinématographique coréenne dans une étroite ruelle urbaine ensoleillée, bordée de bâtiments en briques, d'unités de climatisation, de palettes en bois et d'une supérette GS25 bien visible. Une jeune femme athlétique aux longs cheveux blonds attachés en une haute queue de cheval porte un haut de sport rose à manches longues, près du corps, avec un décolleté carré profond, assorti à un legging de sport rose ajusté et à des baskets de sport roses impeccables. Son visage, son cou et le haut de sa poitrine présentent une légère pellicule de sueur, soulignant qu'elle vient de terminer son entraînement, tandis que son expression reste calme, concentrée et déterminée. Elle fait face à un groupe de jeunes hommes agressifs vêtus de survêtements et de sweats à capuche noirs. Sans hésitation, elle fonce vers eux en utilisant des techniques d'arts martiaux précises et puissantes, enchaînant coups de poing ultra-rapides, coups de pied sautés, coups de coude, attaques de genou, balayages et projections parfaitement exécutés, mettant hors de combat chaque assaillant un par un avec une chorégraphie fluide et réaliste. La caméra portée suit de près l'action dans l'étroite ruelle avec des mouvements dynamiques, un léger tremblement de caméra, un flou de mouvement réaliste et des changements de perspective rapides qui accentuent l'intensité de chaque impact. Les corps s'écrasent sur le pavé à mesure que le combat s'intensifie avec une dynamique cinématographique. En arrière-plan, trois écolières coréennes en uniforme soigné se tiennent près de l'entrée du GS25, observant la confrontation dans un silence stupéfait. La lumière naturelle vive projette des ombres nettes sur les murs de briques et les palettes empilées, soulignant la poussière, les débris et les détails environnementaux réalistes. La séquence se termine lorsque le dernier assaillant s'effondre à côté des palettes empilées. La femme marque une pause, ajuste calmement sa posture de combat, remet en place son haut de sport rose et s'éloigne avec assurance dans la ruelle ensoleillée. Style live-action hyper-réaliste, esthétique de film d'action coréen, chorégraphie à haute énergie, détails faciaux nets, mouvements physiquement précis, travail de caméra portée cinématographique, éclairage naturel, textures réalistes et qualité de long-métrage.
```

### No. 66: Miniature YouTube - Affiche du scandale baroque de Telemann

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Une affiche dramatique de style miniature d'enquête espagnole révélant Telemann comme le véritable compositeur derrière un chef-d'œuvre baroque caché.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Objectif : Créer une affiche de musique historique dramatique de style miniature YouTube d'enquête sur un scandale de paternité baroque caché, centrée sur {argument name="composer name" default="Telemann"}.

Toile : Composition horizontale large 16:9, fond noir profond avec une texture de collage granuleuse, contraste élevé, éclairage cinématographique, accents néon magenta et cyan, couches de parchemin vieilli et de papier déchiré. Donnez-lui l'aspect d'une couverture éditoriale sensationnelle pour une chronique Substack.

Disposition : Le tiers gauche contient le titre principal en espagnol dans une typographie majuscule blanche vieillie très grande : « NOS MINTIERON. » Au-dessus, un petit texte magenta espacé indique « UNA COLUMNA DE SUBSTACK » avec un petit emblème en forme de soleil doré contenant un « T ». Sous le titre, ajoutez un texte espagnol empilé plus petit : « DURANTE AÑOS, LOS EXPERTOS » puis une emphase magenta « OCULTARON LA VERDAD » puis « SOBRE UNA OBRA MAESTRA DEL BARROCO. » En bas, placez une note beige déchirée avec une icône X entourée en magenta et le texte « UN AFICIONADO SE LLEVÓ EL CRÉDITO. » En bas à gauche, ajoutez un immense texte en écriture manuscrite magenta « TELEMANN » et en dessous un petit texte blanc « ERA EL VERDADERO COMPOSITOR. »

Sujet central : Un portrait gravé détaillé d'un compositeur baroque du XVIIIe siècle, vue de face aux trois quarts, perruque poudrée bouclée, expression solennelle, manteau sombre orné de broderies dorées. Ajoutez une lumière cyan sur le côté gauche de ses cheveux et une lumière magenta sur le côté droit, avec un fin halo doré ou un arc de mesure radial derrière sa tête. Incluez les petites initiales manuscrites « G. P. T. » et un petit croquis de couronne dorée près du portrait.

Collage côté droit : Utilisez exactement 7 éléments de preuve principaux : 1) une grande page de manuscrit vieillie et déchirée en haut à droite avec une partition, des barres de censure noires, des marques de correction rouges et un tampon rouge indiquant « CORRECCIONES » ; 2) un texte manuscrit rouge-brun sur cette page indiquant « Editado por un aficionado » ; 3) une loupe au-dessus de la page montrant le texte manuscrit « no por él. » avec une phrase soulignée et un X en dessous ; 4) une bande de parchemin déchirée au milieu brillant d'un or chaud, signée « G. P. Telemann » dans une élégante calligraphie noire avec un petit soleil au-dessus ; 5) une note manuscrite sur papier sombre près du bas indiquant « La verdad estaba en la partitura » ; 6) un sceau de cire rouge estampillé d'un « T » à côté d'une grande plume d'oie grise ; 7) un corps de violon en bois recadré entrant depuis le bord extrême droit.

Détails visuels supplémentaires : Ajoutez de subtiles lignes de portée musicale, des rayures d'encre, de la poussière, des fissures, des éclaboussures de peinture magenta, un cercle de forme d'onde sarcelle près du centre inférieur, des bords déchirés et des ombres de papier superposées. Utilisez des reflets chauds dramatiques sur le parchemin et des accents néon froids sur le portrait. Le texte espagnol doit être lisible et placé comme décrit.

Contraintes de style : Miniature de documentaire d'enquête sombre, collage éditorial grunge, portrait gravé réaliste mélangé à un design graphique moderne, pas de logos supplémentaires, pas de filigrane, pas de texte supplémentaire au-delà des phrases espagnoles spécifiées.
```

Style visuel : Visuel clé d'anime ultra-détaillé mélangé à un design publicitaire technologique brillant, une typographie japonaise audacieuse, un lettrage de titre cinématographique usé, un éclairage de contour vif, des superpositions d'interface holographique, des reflets d'objectif, une composition diagonale rapide et une sensation d'affiche d'action intense. Créez une affiche verticale 4:5 dynamique pour influenceur tech dans un style cyber-bleu brillant. Un jeune homme d'Asie de l'Est aux cheveux noirs courts, portant des lunettes rectangulaires noires et affichant une expression de surprise, bouche ouverte, se penche vers l'appareil photo depuis la droite, vêtu d'un t-shirt noir uni. Il tient un grand jeton bleu circulaire lumineux près de l'objectif avec sa main gauche au premier plan en bas à gauche, le faisant paraître surdimensionné ; le jeton possède un bord vitreux transparent, des reflets bleu néon et un logo géométrique blanc en relief composé de trois blocs distincts sur sa face. Sa main droite pointe vers le jeton depuis la droite. Utilisez une perspective selfie grand angle extrême, une forte distorsion de profondeur, un éclairage contrasté net et un fond noir profond rempli de traînées de lumière bleu électrique, de reflets d'objectif et de lignes de mouvement. Ajoutez exactement 3 éléments textuels visibles : un immense titre en chinois bleu métallique 3D en haut à gauche en arrière-plan indiquant {argument name="background Chinese headline" default="大力"}, un grand titre en italique blanc et bleu en bas indiquant {argument name="model title" default="Qwen3.8"}, et un texte en chinois blanc en gras en bas à droite indiquant {argument name="test label" default="实测"}. Rendez toute la typographie extrudée, brillante, biseautée et illuminée par une lumière bleue sur les bords. La composition doit évoquer une miniature YouTube à haute énergie ou une annonce de test technique sur les réseaux sociaux, ultra nette, cinématographique, légèrement surréaliste, sans texte supplémentaire, sans filigrane.
```

### No. 72: Bande dessinée / Storyboard - Overworked Office Hamster Manga

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

A monochrome six-panel Japanese manga diary page showing a cute hamster salaryman being interrupted during lunch at the office.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Goal: Create a black-and-white Japanese manga diary page about an overworked office hamster employee, in a humorous slice-of-life tone. Use detailed pencil/ink shading, screentone texture, thick black panel borders, expressive manga speech balloons, and a clean modern office background.

Canvas: Vertical manga page, approximately 2:3 aspect ratio, monochrome grayscale, high-resolution print look. Arrange exactly 6 panels: three narrow panels across the top row, two panels across the middle row, and one large dramatic panel spanning the full bottom row.

Setting and main character: The protagonist is a small round fluffy hamster office worker with shiny black eyes, tiny paws, soft fur, and a serious but cute expression. The office has rows of desks, monitors, swivel chairs, fluorescent ceiling lights, filing cabinets, glass entrance doors, a landline telephone, and an analog wall clock. The visual humor comes from treating the hamster like a normal salaryman.

Panel layout and content:
1. Top-left panel: Empty office after hours. The hamster has collapsed into a melted exhausted heap on the floor beside a small low desk with stationery. Add steam-like fatigue lines rising from the body. Vertical label reads {argument name="four hours later label" default="4時間経過"}.
2. Top-middle panel: Same office, one hour after arriving. The hamster sits upright at a desk, still cute but blank and tired, with papers and a pencil cup. Vertical label reads {argument name="one hour later label" default="1時間経過"}.
3. Top-right panel: Morning entrance scene by glass office doors. The hamster walks in carrying a small black business bag and politely greets the office. Include a speech balloon reading {argument name="morning greeting" default="おはようございまーす"}. Vertical label reads {argument name="third obon day label" default="お盆三日目"}.
4. Middle-left panel: The hamster holds an oversized black landline phone receiver with both paws, standing beside the phone base. It looks earnest and professional. Include two speech balloons: one calm balloon saying {argument name="phone answer text" default="はい、〇〇のハムです"}, and one jagged loud reply balloon from the other party saying 「あー！ハムさんいたぁ!!」.
5. Middle-right panel: Lunch break at the desk. The hamster happily eats noodles from a bowl with chopsticks; steam rises from the bowl. A large landline phone sits in the foreground, and an analog wall clock shows noon. Add sound effect text 「プルルルル」 near the phone. Vertical label reads 「昼休み」.
6. Bottom full-width panel: Dramatic dark background with strong radial speed lines and heavy contrast. The hamster is now angry and intense, gripping the oversized phone receiver, with a manga anger mark on its forehead. On the left, add a large white burst caption reading 「このための出勤とかマジでふざけんな」. On the right, add an ornate flower-decorated thought/speech bubble containing vertical Japanese complaint text: 「あのぉ〜、急ぎ対応してほしいものがあってぇ。お昼中なのにごめんなさい。えっとぉ、私の作った資料ダメ出しされちゃってぇ……ハムさんに直して欲しいんですぅ。あっ、私お昼中なんで、お昼終わるまでにください！」

Style constraints: Use authentic seinen/josei manga diary aesthetics, grayscale pencil crosshatching, soft fur detail, office realism, readable Japanese lettering, and strong comedic timing. Keep exactly 6 panels and exactly the listed text elements; do not add color, extra characters, watermark, or extra captions.
```

Canvas: Wide horizontal 3:1 character sheet on a neutral medium-gray studio background, high-resolution photorealistic fantasy concept art with soft cinematic lighting and sharp fabric/leather detail.

Layout: Use exactly 11 visible views arranged across the canvas: 4 small stacked portrait studies on the far left, 1 large central front-facing three-quarter body portrait, 5 smaller full-body turnaround/action reference views in the middle-right, and 1 large close-up face portrait on the far right.

Visual style: Photorealistic AAA game character concept sheet, realistic materials, detailed leather stitching and armor paneling, clean studio presentation, subtle depth of field on the close-up, dramatic but controlled lighting, no weapons visible, no text labels, no watermark, no extra characters. Emphasize the {argument name="mood" default="alert warrior stance"} and the cultural-fantasy warrior identity without adding modern accessories.

![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Creates charming 3D paper-craft dioramas of travel scenes, perfect for storybook aesthetics and nostalgic miniature art.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a premium handcrafted 3D paper-craft travel diorama centered around a little girl and her beloved doll on a magical journey.

adorable young girl doll character sitting on the upper edge of the diorama with her favorite small doll companion beside her. She wears a charming vintage travel outfit — a soft knitted cardigan, pleated skirt, little boots, cute hat, and a tiny backpack. She holds a miniature travel journal or map, while her doll is dressed in a matching outfit and carries a tiny suitcase.

The main scene inside the dimensional frame should represent {argument name="destination" default="Paris"} as a magical miniature world. Include its most recognizable architecture and landscapes, but reinterpret everything as handcrafted toys and paper sculptures: tiny buildings, colorful streets, flowers, trees, bridges, cafés, lanterns, local decorations, and a small pathway leading into the scene.

Add charming storytelling details:

The girl’s doll sitting beside her as her travel companion
Tiny suitcase covered with travel stickers
Miniature teddy bear peeking from the backpack
A tiny camera hanging around the girl’s neck
Small postcards and stamps scattered around the frame
A miniature paper airplane flying through the scene
A tiny map with a dotted route
Handcrafted flowers and plants around the foreground

Use a soft pastel vintage palette of dusty pink, warm cream, terracotta, muted teal, sage green, peach, and warm brown.

The entire artwork should feel like a luxury handcrafted children’s storybook mixed with a vintage travel scrapbook, using layered cardstock, paper quilling, embossed paper, miniature clay details, fabric textures, stitched leather accents, and delicate hand-painted surfaces.

Make the girl and her doll especially charming and expressive, with a cute collectible-doll aesthetic, oversized curious eyes, soft handcrafted facial details, and a warm adventurous personality.

Add a large raised plaque at the bottom reading:

{argument name="destination" default="Paris"}
{argument name="country" default="France"}

Surround the composition with subtle travel elements such as a compass, postage stamps, luggage tags, leaves, postcards, and a dotted travel route.

Soft warm studio lighting, realistic miniature shadows, tactile paper fibers, shallow depth of field, premium product photography, highly detailed handcrafted textures, whimsical cinematic atmosphere, elegant composition, cozy nostalgic feeling, museum-quality miniature diorama, 8K.

Avoid: photorealistic humans, plastic-looking 3D characters, excessive text, clutter, harsh colors, modern objects, generic fantasy scenery.
```

### No. 78: Bande dessinée / Storyboard - Oriental Papercut Art of Sacred Temple Bridge

![Language-ZH](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-ZH-blue)

#### 📖 Description

A sophisticated prompt for generating a three-dimensional layered papercut illustration, depicting a woman crossing a celestial bridge toward a glowing paper temple with intricate carving textures.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
9:16 vertical version, modern oriental female dreamscape 3D papercut art, modern oriental papercut art, layered paper cut illustration, dimensional paper sculpture, intricate paper carving, paper diorama, layered shadow box, epic oriental papercraft fantasy, poetic female dreamscape, contemporary oriental art poster. The theme of the work is 'She crosses a suspended sky bridge towards the center of the temple.' The core narrative of the scene is: a young oriental woman alone embarks on a long paper-art sky bridge floating above a sea of clouds, with a giant glowing paper-sculpture temple at the end of the bridge. She moves forward step by step, finally deciding to enter the center that only appeared in her dreams. The entire scene emphasizes a pilgrimage-like sense of progression, a vast high-altitude world, and the epic momentum of a paper-art mythological scene. The main character is an adult oriental woman with a visual age of about 25, bright, brave, quiet, and firm, with a sense of solemnity when facing the unknown temple. The character has a soft oval face, clear almond eyes, and gazes intently at the distant temple with a calm and determined expression. The character is not the main large-scale close-up of the scene but an emotional anchor in a huge world, and the proportions must be significantly small. Character hairstyle and headwear: long black hair naturally loose, the ends of the hair blown by the wind into slender flowing paper ribbons, wearing light gold leaves and small star-shaped hair accessories. The headwear is exquisite and light, not complex or tall. The long hair and silk drape extend behind her in the high-altitude wind, forming clear and elegant flowing lines. Character clothing: a layered paper-art long skirt in moon white and cyan-green, the hem unfolded in layers of leaves and cloud patterns, the silk drape slender and light, partially with light gold hollowed patterns. The clothing must have a multi-layered paper structure, fine hollowed edges, paper fibers, slight creases, real paper thickness, and a high-degree of handmade cutting feel. The hem and drape extend in the wind on the bridge, but do not block the perspective direction of the bridge. The dream carrier is: a suspended paper-art sky bridge. Dream imagery includes: sky bridge, cloud sea, temple, flying birds, distant mountains, glowing cloud layers. All bridges, temples, clouds, mountains, birds, and characters must be unified in a high-quality 3D paper sculpture language; realistic characters with paper-cut backgrounds or ordinary CG architecture are not allowed. The overall paper-cut structure adopts: a surrounding dreamscape style. Composition and camera requirements: the camera is located on the bridge surface, using a low-angle or eye-level shot, high-angle top-down shots cannot be used. The line of sight extends along the bridge surface toward the distant temple, forming a strong single-point perspective and epic depth. The sky bridge extends from the foreground at the bottom of the screen to the far center, leading directly to the temple. The bridge width is moderate but visually extremely long, with exquisite hollowed railings, suspension chains, carvings, and paper-art components on both sides. The character is located in a small position in the middle-lower part of the bridge, facing away from or semi-facing away from the camera, walking towards the distant temple. The character must be significantly smaller than the bridge and the temple to highlight the oppressive sense of the scene and the scale of the bridge. The main subject of the scene is an extremely long and narrow suspended paper-art sky bridge. The bridge body floats above an infinite sea of clouds, and the bridge surface is composed of silver-white, moon-white, and light-gold details, with rich oriental hollowed patterns, paper-sculpted railings, layered structures, and translucent edges. The bridge is not an ordinary small bridge but like a sacred bridge leading to the center of a divine realm, with exaggerated length and strong depth, making the viewer feel the distance and the sense of high-altitude danger. In the distance is a huge, multi-layered glowing paper-sculpted temple. The volume of the temple is much larger than the person and even much larger than the bridge entrance porch, possessing a clear sense of oppression and divine scale. The temple is composed of multiple halls, arches, towers, corridors, and a central glowing entrance, with a complex and exquisite structure, layered and situated between the sea of clouds and the mountains. There is a glowing doorway or main hall entrance at the center of the temple, becoming the final visual focus. The temple should be like the ultimate center in a dream, not a common courtyard building. On both sides of the bridge are a huge sea of clouds and high-altitude white space. The sea of clouds is composed of a large number of paper layers of different thicknesses, forming surging layers and a massive sense of volume. Distant paper-art mountains rise from the clouds, the peaks are cyan and misty blue, with fine gold-line hollowed decorations. A few birds may fly by in the high sky to enhance the sense of scale and airspace, but do not stack too many. The overall color scheme is: moon white, cyan-green, silver white as primary colors, with a small amount of light gold accents. Moon white is used for the bridge body, bright parts of the temple, outer layers of character clothing, and highlights of the sea of clouds; cyan-green is used for distant mountains, inner layers of clothing, and partial decorations; silver white is used for temple highlights, bridge surface highlights, and the sky atmosphere; light gold is only used for railing chains, hollowed patterns, clothing outlines, and partial highlights. The overall look must be bright, transparent, and clear, not gray, not darkened, not overall blueish, and not yellowish. Light and paper texture: bright daylight with a blend of cold and warm tones at high altitude illuminates the sky bridge and the temple, the edges of the bridge surface are slightly translucent, the shadow layers of the temple paper are clear, and the paper fibers and layered structures are exquisite and obvious. The distant temple entrance emits a soft but strong light, extending toward the foreground along the bridge. The bridge railings, character's shoulders, hair strands, and skirt edges have soft rim lighting. The paper surface must have clearly visible fiber textures, manual cutting marks, slight creases, layered shadows, and translucent edges, overall looking like a high-quality museum-grade paper sculpture art installation. The atmosphere of the image is: epic, solemn, sacred, vast, quiet, and firm. The focus is not on exaggerated character postures, but on creating a strong sense of pilgrimage and oppression through the extremely long sky bridge, giant temple, massive sea of clouds, and high-altitude space. The scene must look like a paper-art mythological epic scene, not an ordinary dreamscape.
```

### No. 80: Bande dessinée / Storyboard - Giant Anime Maid Over Shanghai FPS Scene

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Generates a cinematic first-person shooter view of rainy Shanghai invaded by a colossal anime maid giantess, ideal for surreal game screenshot concepts.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Goal: Create a cinematic first-person shooter screenshot in Shanghai at night, blending gritty realistic military-game visuals with a surreal giant anime maid looming over the skyline.

Canvas: Wide 16:9 frame, dark rainy night, high-contrast blue-gray color grading, wet lens grime, film grain, subtle scratches, dramatic storm clouds, reflections on the river.

Scene and layout: The viewer stands on a riverside battlefield facing the Pudong skyline across dark water. The Oriental Pearl Tower is clearly visible on the far left, with illuminated skyscrapers and neon signs along the waterfront. In the lower right foreground, show a realistic black scoped assault rifle held in first-person view, rain-speckled and aimed toward the skyline. Add fires, smoke plumes, small explosions, and city lights reflected in the water. A helicopter with a bright searchlight appears in the upper right sky.

Main subject: A colossal semi-transparent anime giantess appears behind and above the skyscrapers, scaled so her head and shoulders dominate the sky. She is {argument name="character name" default="an original anime maid giantess"} with {argument name="hair color" default="pink-purple twin-tail hair with blue streaks"}, large purple eyes, small open mouth, and a mischievous or threatening expression. She wears a frilly maid-style headpiece and sailor-maid outfit with dark blue ribbons. Her left hand reaches forward toward the viewer in extreme foreshortening, with the palm and fingers huge in the foreground, as if trying to grab the city or the player. Make her softly luminous and slightly transparent, like a massive holographic or supernatural apparition in the rain.

HUD and readable overlays: Include exactly 5 distinct game-interface elements: 1 square minimap in the lower left, 1 centered crosshair/weapon status cluster near the bottom, 1 ammo counter panel in the lower right showing large numbers similar to “21 / 84”, 1 small status label above the ammo panel, and 1 row of small icons at the top right. Include exactly 2 centered Chinese subtitle lines near the bottom: {argument name="first subtitle line" default="汉娜：大家快看天上！"} and {argument name="second subtitle line" default="爱尔兰佬：妈的，这是啥玩意！"}. Add a small Chinese objective label near the minimap reading {argument name="objective label" default="抵达撤离点"}.

Counted visible elements to include: exactly 1 giant anime maid, exactly 1 oversized reaching hand, exactly 1 helicopter with searchlight, exactly 1 first-person scoped rifle, exactly 1 Oriental Pearl Tower, exactly 2 subtitle lines, and exactly 5 HUD/interface groups. Do not add extra characters in the foreground.

Visual style: Photorealistic AAA military FPS screenshot with realistic rain, wet surfaces, smoky battlefield atmosphere, cinematic lighting, and authentic UI overlays; contrast the realistic war scene with a crisp anime character rendered as a gigantic translucent figure.
```

5. Bottom-right panel: the character drinks a large foamy beer from a tilted glass with both hands, cheeks slightly puffed, sweat drop on the face, blue vertical speed-line background, wooden tabletop at the bottom. Show splashes or foam droplets near the glass.

### No. 96: Marketing produit - Sarah Nabila Cinematic Double Exposure

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

A sophisticated prompt for creating a double-exposure portrait poster that blends high-fashion editorial aesthetics with futuristic typography and studio lighting.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a premium cinematic double-exposure portrait poster of the subject in a {argument name="background style" default="clean, soft beige studio environment"}, 4:5 aspect ratio. Use a sophisticated visual style combining Luxury Fashion Campaign, Apple Commercial, Premium Editorial Photography, and Hollywood Cinematic Poster aesthetics.

Feature a large, highly realistic side-profile portrait of the subject facing left, occupying approximately 70–75% of the frame height. The expression should be calm, confident, and elegant, with extremely sharp and detailed facial features, realistic skin texture, natural proportions, and refined editorial lighting. Behind the portrait, add strong horizontal motion-blur streaks moving toward the right, creating speed and dynamic energy while remaining clean, controlled, and premium.

In the foreground, include a full-body version of the exact same subject, approximately 30–35% of the overall frame height. Both versions must clearly be the same person with identical facial identity, facial features, skin tone, hairstyle, body shape, and overall visual characteristics. The double-exposure effect should feel like one seamless identity rather than two separate images.

The full-body subject wears a premium casual beige-toned outfit consisting of a relaxed-fit beige overshirt or jacket, neutral inner shirt, light-wash loose-fit jeans, modern casual sneakers, and transparent-frame modern glasses. Pose the subject naturally and confidently with both hands inside the trouser pockets, one leg naturally crossed in front of the other, and the body slightly leaning in a relaxed fashion-editorial posture. The head and eyes should be subtly directed toward the typography rather than looking directly at the camera.

Behind the full-body subject, integrate large futuristic typography reading exactly “{argument name="name" default="SARAH NABILA"}”. The typography should be bold, futuristic, modern, premium, slightly transparent, layered, and dimensional, with parts naturally hidden behind the subject to create convincing foreground-background integration. It must feel embedded into the composition rather than appearing as flat ordinary text.

Add minimal futuristic graphic direction around the composition: subtle geometric shapes, thin technical lines, translucent panels, fine grid details, minimal interface elements, futuristic markers, layered graphics, and subtle depth effects. The pot is 70% full of clear light-gold syrup, precisely containing eight pieces of cut snow pear, six pieces of transparent rock sugar, and three dried osmanthus flowers. A transparent glass spoon scoops a piece of pear from the right, stopping about 4cm above the rim of the pot, with the syrup backflow controlled at 3cm. The center of the pot is at 62% horizontal and 64% vertical, occupying about 38% of the frame width. The area from 7% to 42% horizontal and 9% to 47% vertical on the left remains a warm white text zone. The foreground contains only half a fresh snow pear with the cut side facing up, while the background features a soft white sheer curtain; do not add red dates, wolfberries, or tremella. The camera is 12cm above the middle of the pot, using a 90mm macro lens shooting from the front left at a 14-degree angle from a distance of 72cm. The focus is locked on the pear fibers in the spoon, the transparency of the syrup, and the edge of the glass pot, with the lid remaining recognizable. A large window-shaped soft light of about 5500K from the back left penetrates the syrup and pear meat, while a warm white reflector at the front right restores the glass contours. Two strands of very faint steam rise towards the window. The color scheme is 52% warm white background, 18% transparent light-gold syrup, 20% pear milk white, 5% glass gray, and 5% dark brown text. Pear meat should be soft and slightly translucent but retain its chunk shape; syrup should be clear and low-viscosity, not a thick jam or milky soup. Text is limited to brand name "{argument name="brand name" default="Pear Stewed Slowly"}", product name "{argument name="product name" default="Rock Sugar Stewed Pear"}", main copy "{argument name="main copy" default="Pear fragrance stewed slowly, sweetness falls clearly"}", and English sub-title "ROCK SUGAR STEWED PEAR". The main text on the left uses a dark brown fine Song font in three lines, brand name at the top, product name and English at bottom left. Lock one pot, one lid, eight pear chunks, six rock sugar pieces, three osmanthus flowers, one glass spoon, and back-left window light; do not add herbs, a second pot, excessive condensation, thick steam, or floating pear chunks.
```
