![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)

#### 📖 Description

A highly technical fashion generation prompt for a classical girlie look, detailing the fabric textures, seams, and construction of a mesh knit crop top and a checkered A-line mini skirt.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Wardrobe: Top: One solid muted cocoa-taupe sleeveless mock-neck crop top made from a fine-gauge stretch mesh knit with tightly regular micro-openings, a lightweight semi-sheer section, a smooth low-luster face, soft close drape, and resilient multidirectional recovery. Cut a close straight-to-lightly-tapered body ending at the upper waist, with matching front and rear lower-edge levels and minimal bulk. Form a narrow circular self-fabric mock neck whose finished height is between one-tenth and one-sixth of the shoulder-to-hem length, using a double-layer return and one low-bulk rear join. Continue two narrow shoulder sections into high-cut sleeveless armholes and uninterrupted front and rear body panels. Finish both armhole circuits with narrow self-fabric turned bindings less than one-tenth of the shoulder-section width, join the body through two clean side seams, and complete the lower circumference with one narrow straight double-turn hem. Use the knit recovery and complete neck opening for a pull-on construction without exterior fastening. Keep the mesh scale evenly legible, the semi-sheer density consistent, the mock neck softly upright, the armhole edges flat, and the lower hem free of heavy opaque bulk. Bottom: One camel-tan wrap-front mini skirt made from medium-weight brushed twill with a compact diagonal weave, a short soft nap, a matte low-luster surface, firm shape retention, and rounded low-bulk folds; confine espresso brown, ivory, and muted dusty-rose to the woven geometric check and matte black to two front buttons. Cut a high-rise upper-thigh mini length with a close waist and upper hip, then widen gently into a compact A-line hem whose laid-flat width is between one-and-one-fifth and one-and-two-fifths of the top-edge width. Form a clean curved faced upper edge without a separate exterior waistband. Build smooth front and rear bodies with two equal rear waist darts, one at product-right and one at product-left, two side seams, and one full-height product-left front overlap panel spanning between one-half and two-thirds of the front width; keep its vertical free edge straight from the top edge to the hem and its underlap continuous behind it. Cover every front, overlap, side, and rear panel with a woven repeat of large upright camel rectangles bounded by single espresso lines and paired ivory lines, then add one fine dusty-rose pinstripe only to alternating vertical and horizontal corridors so the rose intersections shift by one cell on every second row; each complete repeat spans between one-quarter and one-third of the laid-flat front width, with straight orthogonal lines and clean registration across darts, seams, and the overlap. Mount two circular matte-black four-hole buttons in one vertical product-left-front line, each between one-tenth and one-eighth of the waist-to-hem depth in diameter; place the upper station within the upper quarter of that depth and the lower station between one-half and two-thirds of the depth. Secure the overlap through two matching reinforced buttonholes on the underlap, keeping both stations fully engaged and the panel flat, and finish the complete lower edge with one narrow turned hem. Preserve the short brushed nap, readable diagonal weave, crisp A-line outline, stable overlap depth, aligned geometric repeat, flat button seating, and material-correct hem thickness. Footwear: One matching pair of predominantly black combat boots built from medium-thick semi-matte coated leather-like upper material with a fine shallow grain and broad flexible crease response, black flat woven cord, silver-tone metal, and deep black molded rubber. Each boot is knee-high with a tall straight shaft, a broad rounded toe, a substantial forefoot platform, and a slightly deeper rear block, with shaft volume remaining open and stable near the top before compressing into broad slouch folds through the lower shaft and ankle. A wide straight topline forms the shaft opening and retains a clean finished edge. The upper consists of tall side and rear shaft panels joined to a rounded forefoot, broad vamp, quarters, and a central tongue; the vamp stays close while the shaft carries relaxed vertical volume. Paired rows of black lace holes run from the forefoot into the lower shaft and hold flat black laces, while two separate narrow wrap straps encircle each boot, one near the upper shaft and one at the ankle, each ending at its own compact rectangular silver-tone buckle. Tonal topstitching defines the panel joins, and the sole forms a thick continuous sidewall above a deeply cut lugged contact face. The laces close in compact tied bows and both buckle straps remain fastened through their rectangular frames. Both boots are identical anatomical mirror counterparts with the same panel layout, lace path, strap count, buckle placement, shaft height, slouch distribution, toe shape, and sole stack. Preserve the coated fine grain through the broad folds, keep the two buckle circuits physically separate from the lace closure, and maintain crisp sole edges, deep lug relief, firm toe volume, and material-correct seam bulk. Bag: Styling — highest priority: - Bag carry: Hang the strap from the wearer-right shoulder only without crossing the chest, and let the bag body lie flat along the right flank between the lower ribs and outer hip. Let the strap bear the full weight and keep both hands off the bag and strap. Keep the complete strap and bag body entirely outside and visibly separate from every garment, routing the strap over, never beneath or through, the outermost clothing. Do not pass it through any neckline, sleeve, knit opening, seam, or rear panel, and do not merge, attach, weave, replace, or reinterpret any bag material as part of a garment. - Finish — Natural: Adjust only material-correct slack and body contact along the exact selected strap route. Keep the selected route and all attachment points exact while allowing limited material-correct slack and natural body contact. One warm-ivory soft gathered chain-strap pouch bag made from lightweight lightly padded matte woven fabric, constructed as one medium rounded crescent-like body with softly tapered upper corners, a broad curved lower edge, shallow expandable side depth, and controlled flexible volume. Gather the complete upper body into one reinforced top-edge opening, producing repeated soft vertical folds that release through the front, rear, and side surfaces without rigid quilting lines. Add one long warm-gold-tone curb-chain strap attached through two small polished gold-tone D-rings and reinforced matching fabric tabs at the upper sides, preserving identical attachment height and hardware construction on both ends. Complete the front, rear, side panels, curved base, gathered upper edge, reinforced top opening, chain strap, D-rings, attachment tabs, hidden seam allowances, and internal soft support as one coherent bag with no visible exterior closure hardware, exterior pocket, readable branding, detachable charm, keychain, plush ornament, mascot, or additional decoration. Preserve lightweight textile response, softly padded body volume, even gather distribution, clean upper-edge reinforcement, secure chain anchoring, low seam bulk, and restrained warm-gold reflections. Necklace: One short silver fine-chain necklace built as one continuous closed circuit with a shallow curved ornament centered on one product axis. Keep most of the circuit as a slender uniform chain and confine the ornament to a short lower arc substantially shorter than the complete chain path. Construct the ornament from a closely spaced row of uniform short rounded cylindrical beads aligned radially to form a gentle crescent, connecting both ornament terminals directly into compact polished chain links. Maintain bilateral orn
```

### No. 108: Image principale du e-commerce - Pinstriped Button-Up Shirt Design

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)

#### 📖 Description

A highly technical fashion prompt detailing the construction, styling, and embroidery of a cropped pinstriped shirt with rolled sleeves and botanical threadwork.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
ワードローブ： トップス： スタイリング： - フロントクロージャー：フロント中央のクロージャーの上部約3分の1だけを開け、胸より下のクロージャーはすべて裾まで留めたままにする。上部の開口部は左右対称に保つ。開口部を下部まで広げたり、上部を閉じ直したりしない。 - 袖：各袖口を外側にちょうど2回折り返し、両腕にダブルロールのバンドを作る。両方のバンドを前腕の中ほど、肘の折り目より下で手首より上の位置に配置する。他のスタイリング指示で衣服が肩から下がった場合でも、この巻き数と高さを維持する。袖を肘まで押し上げたり、袖口を隠したりしない。 - 仕上げ — ナチュラル： - フロントクロージャー：選択した上部開口部のみを外し、下の留め具はすべて留めたままにする。その間隔を変更せず、固定された留め状態に隣接するパネルのドレープのみを調整する。 - 袖：選択した数、位置、向き、レイヤー順序を変更しない。固定された袖の高さより下のロール構造のみを調整し、元の袖口を保持する。適度な重なりとわずかな非対称性を持つ柔らかい折り目を使用する。 淡いスカイブルーと白の垂直ピンストライプの長袖クロップドボタンアップシャツ。軽量なポプリン風の織物で、パリッとしたマットな質感。ボクシーなシルエットで、ウエストより上で終わる裾を持つ。白いポインテッドカラーと、小さな白いボタンが並ぶフロント前立てを備える。袖山に軽いギャザーがあり、広いピンストライプのバレルカフスはボタンで閉じられている。左胸にパッチポケットがあり、全体に白い植物風の刺繍やオープンワークが施されている。左胸ポケットには、半透明の花びらのアップリケやビーズを用いた立体的な装飾が集中している。ジッパーや余計な装飾のない、一貫したデザインのシャツである。 ボトムス：淡いクールグレーのハイライズフレアミニスカート。中重量のコンパクトなポンチニット製で、マットな質感。太もも上部丈のスケーターシルエット。伸縮性のあるアッパーバンドを備え、複数のパネルとシームラインで構成されている。ポケットやジッパーのないプルオンタイプで、素材の厚みと放射状のフレアを維持している。 レッグウェア：温かみのある白のショートクルーソックス。上質な垂直リブニット製で、左右対称の設計。リブ編みのアッパーカフと補強されたかかと・つま先を持ち、装飾のないシンプルなデザイン。適度な不透明度とストレッチ性を備えている。 フットウェア：温かみのあるアイボリーのロートップチャンキーレザースニーカー。丸いつま先と厚いプラットフォームソールが特徴。アッパーはスエード調の素材とオフホワイトのメッシュを組み合わせ、幾何学的なオーバーレイを配置。オフホワイトの靴紐が左右対称に通されている。頑丈なラグトレッドを備えた厚底ソールを持ち、細部まで一貫した配色と質感で仕上げられている。 バッグ： スタイリング： - バッグの持ち方：ストラップを左肩からのみ下げ、バッグ本体を左脇腹に置く。胸を横切らせず、手は添えない。バッグとストラップは衣服の外側に完全に露出させ、衣服の下や中を通さないこと。 - 仕上げ — ナチュラル：選択したストラップのルートに沿って、素材に忠実な緩みと体への接触のみを調整する。選択したルートとすべての取り付けを維持する。
```

```
Cinematic Pepsi energy drink commercial, ultra-realistic product photography style. A sleek blue and silver Pepsi can standing upright on a reflective dark surface with a soft gradient grey background. Condensation water droplets cover the can. A strong male hand reaches in and pulls the ice-cold Pepsi can out of a metal ice bucket filled with clear ice cubes. Extreme close-up of water droplets on the metallic surface of the can. The pull-tab opens with a burst of cold mist and vapor rising from the top. Golden amber liquid pours from the can into a clear glass filled with ice cubes, creating bubbles and fizz. A shirtless, extremely muscular, fit young man with sharp jawline and short brown hair drinks from the Red Bull can, eyes closed in satisfaction, then smiles confidently while holding the can. Dynamic action shot of the Red Bull can floating in mid-air surrounded by exploding ice cubes, water splash, and light rays. Final shot: Pepsi logo (two red Pepsi and yellow sun) appears above the cold, wet Pepsi can on a reflective surface. High-end commercial lighting, sharp details, cold blue and silver color grading, steam and condensation effects, 8K, photorealistic.
```

### No. 117: Image principale du e-commerce - Page produit e-commerce pour thé Oolong style Zen

![Language-ZH](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-ZH-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Un prompt méticuleux pour générer une page produit e-commerce haut de gamme, optimisée pour mobile, pour une marque de thé conceptuelle, suivant un parcours de conversion structuré avec une esthétique Zen.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Concevez une page produit e-commerce haut de gamme, optimisée pour mobile, pour la marque conceptuelle '{argument name="brand name" default="LANXU"}', ciblant un parcours de conversion complet pour le {argument name="product name" default="Thé Oolong de Haute Montagne"}. Le produit est un coffret cadeau composé de deux boîtes, chaque boîte contenant {argument name="net content" default="100g"}.
Maintenez une cohérence stricte : marque 'LANXU', deux boîtes à thé cylindriques en métal (env. 135 mm de hauteur, 78 mm de diamètre) avec une finition mate gris roche et des couvercles en cuivre antique. Le logo vertical de la marque 'LANXU' et les étiquettes des produits doivent être identiques sur les deux boîtes. Système visuel : 'Luxe Zen + Organique Naturel', concept central 'La brume de montagne dans une tasse'. Palette de couleurs : gris roche, blanc brume, vert pousse, ambre thé et cuivre oxydé. Langage graphique : contours de vallée abstraits, fine brume matinale et couches de thé en diffusion. Typographie : Songti moderne et sans-serif épuré avec de larges espaces blancs.
Mise en page : env. 1242 px de largeur, format vertical long (ratio 1:5). Sept modules continus :
1. 'Valeur Hero' : deux boîtes avec coffret cadeau sur fond de vallée brumeuse abstraite, titre 'La brume de montagne dans une tasse'.
2. 'Pourquoi le déguster' : plans macro des feuilles sèches, feuilles humides et thé ambré limpide, étiquetés 'Profil aromatique : Sous réserve d'évaluation réelle'.
3. 'Origine et Matière première' : pentes de théiers abstraites et détails de récolte, espaces réservés pour l'origine et l'altitude.
4. 'Processus de production' : quatre étapes (Récolte, Flétrissage, Torréfaction, Conditionnement) avec des noms d'espaces réservés.
5. 'Expérience d'infusion' : scène réaliste avec Gaiwan en porcelaine blanche, pichet et thé en train d'être versé ; espaces réservés pour la température et le temps.
6. 'Conditionnement et Spécifications' : montrer clairement la boîte, les deux boîtes, l'état ouvert et l'étiquette '100g x 2 boîtes'.
7. 'Confiance d'achat' : scénario de cadeau, spécifications fictives et informations logistiques.
Style : Direction artistique e-commerce premium combinant photographie commerciale réelle et design graphique raffiné. Tous les éléments doivent avoir des proportions et des points de contact réalistes. Les feuilles de thé ne doivent pas flotter et la brume ne doit pas masquer la visibilité du produit.
Affichez uniquement le texte principal : 'LANXU', 'Oolong de Haute Montagne', 'La brume de montagne dans une tasse', 'Feuilles entières', 'Profil aromatique : Espace réservé', 'Informations sur l'origine : Espace réservé', 'Informations sur le processus : Espace réservé', 'Conseil d'infusion : Espace réservé', '100g x 2 boîtes', 'Informations produit : Espace réservé'.
```

### No. 118: Ressource de jeu - Bird Shaman Character Turnaround

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Creates a four-view T-pose fantasy bird-shaman character sheet for 3D modeling, rigging, or concept art reference.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Goal: Create a clean 3D character design turnaround sheet for a dark fantasy humanoid bird-shaman character, suitable for modeling and rigging reference.

Canvas: Wide horizontal canvas, 16:9 aspect ratio, light warm gray studio background with no props, no shadows beyond soft contact shading, centered full-body figures with generous spacing.

Layout: Show exactly 4 full-body character views in a straight row, all in a rigid T-pose with arms extended horizontally: 1 front view, 2 left side profile view, 3 back view, 4 right side profile view. Keep all four views the same height and scale, evenly spaced, feet aligned on the same baseline.

Character details: The character is {argument name="character archetype" default="a dark fantasy bird-masked tribal shaman"} with a slim humanoid body, black fitted under-suit or dark skin-tight body layer, birdlike plague-doctor mask or long beak helmet, and a crown of thin red-orange feather or tendril plumes emerging from the head. The face is hidden by the mask; the front mask has a long pale bone-colored beak, dark eye openings, and carved ornamental markings. The back view shows a darker head with red tendrils visible from behind.

Outfit: The costume has exactly these visible main clothing pieces: 1 long open sleeveless olive-green robe or coat with split front and pointed tails, 2 short pale green sleeves over the upper arms, 3 black fitted forearm guards or gloves, 4 black leggings, 5 open-toe sandals, 6 layered dark feather collar around the shoulders, 7 pale leaf-like feather necklace around the neck, 8 circular bronze ring ornaments at the waist and knees, 9 hanging strap details at the hips, 10 a vertical laced panel down the upper back. The robe color is {argument name="robe color" default="muted olive green"} with weathered fabric texture and faded salmon-pink circular patterns on the sides and lower back. Use bronze, bone, leather, and dark charcoal materials.

Pose and anatomy: T-pose character sheet, arms perfectly straight and level, palms facing down or slightly inward, legs straight, feet shoulder-width apart. The figure should feel game-ready and sculptural, with consistent proportions across all views.

Visual style: High-quality 3D render, realistic stylized fantasy design, matte materials, subtle worn fabric texture, crisp details, neutral orthographic camera, no dramatic lighting, no text labels, no grid, no watermark. Emphasize symmetry, readable silhouettes, and production-art clarity. Use {argument name="render style" default="realistic stylized 3D game character turnaround"}.
```

### 🐛 GitHub Issue

1. Click [**Soumettre un nouveau prompt**](https://github.com/YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2/issues/new?template=submit-prompt.yml)
2. Remplir le formulaire avec les détails du prompt et l'image
3. Soumettre et attendre l'examen de l'équipe
4. Si approuvé (nous ajouterons le label `approved`), il sera automatiquement synchronisé avec le CMS
5. Votre prompt apparaîtra dans le README dans les 4 heures

**Note :** Nous n'acceptons que les soumissions via GitHub Issues pour assurer le contrôle qualité.

Voir [CONTRIBUTING.md](docs/CONTRIBUTING.md) pour les directives détaillées.
